### Section: Introduction
- **Level**: `intro`
- **Hash**: `2e93cb33c040c0b3485f9e7e8c6b1c54538dedde9e9a33f8c66c7f053022e586`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/what-questions-should-a-school-ask-before-buying-an-ai-detector` (Similarity: 0.8925)

```text
A Technology Director evaluating modern plagiarism software must look for 5 essential technical and operational criteria: Multi-Signal Integrity Architecture (combining web matching, AI detection, and keystroke playback), Certified 1EdTech LTI 1.3 Integration, Ironclad FERPA & SOC 2 Security, Centralized Administrative Analytics, and Transparent Unlimited Licensing.

For school district and university IT leadership, choosing an academic integrity platform is no longer just about buying a simple similarity scanner. In today's digital classroom—where generative AI models, online paraphrasers, and cloud editors are ubiquitous—legacy single-score checkers fail both educators and students. IT leaders must procure a robust, multi-dimensional platform that seamlessly integrates into Canvas and Google Classroom, scales across tens of thousands of users without administrative overhead, and fiercely protects student data privacy.

Below is the definitive evaluation checklist for Chief Technology Officers, IT Directors, and EdTech Procurement Committees.

Checkmark Plagiarism meets every IT benchmark by pairing plagiarism detection with essay writing playback , AI detection , autograding , and integrations with Canvas and Google Classroom .
```

### Section: The 5 Pillars of Modern Plagiarism Checker Procurement
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `0326a1620ad1780ddd6563babd318f48568f8632ae0c1f5a1d93e080d667344e`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/what-questions-should-a-school-ask-before-buying-an-ai-detector` (Similarity: 0.8448)

```text
1. Multi-Signal Detection Architecture

Must combine 90+ billion web page scanning, private peer repository matching, neural AI detection, and 15-second keystroke writing playback.

2. Certified 1EdTech LTI 1.3 Integration

Must support OAuth 2.0 / JWT authentication, SpeedGrader sidebar embeds, two-way automated grade passback, and zero-touch submission webhooks.

3. Ironclad FERPA & SOC 2 Security

Mandates a strict zero-training policy on student data, signed NDPA agreements, AES-256 encryption, and annual SOC 2 Type II / HECVAT verification.

4. Centralized District Analytics

Provides real-time administrative dashboards tracking longitudinal integrity baselines, departmental heatmaps, and course-level anomaly alerts.

5. Predictable, Unlimited Institution-Wide Licensing

Eliminates per-scan limits or surprise credit charges—offering predictable annual district-wide licensing with unlimited scans, autograding, and video replays.
```

### Section: Why Multi-Signal Architecture Saves IT Support Time
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `3d2ad7dcc3c4dc26c6820622766551ad90d0363175af475070ce2d8eeebd32f3`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/how-can-teachers-prioritize-which-assignments-need-manual-review` (Similarity: 0.8358)

```text
Legacy single-score checkers flood district IT and administration with support tickets and disputes:

Eliminates False Positive Grievances: By including Essay Playback directly in reports, 95% of AI score disputes are resolved between student and teacher in seconds without escalating to the principal or IT. 
 Zero Account Provisioning: Single Sign-On (SSO) and LTI 1.3 automatically sync course rosters, meaning IT staff never have to manually upload CSV user spreadsheets. 
 Automated Gradebook Sync: Two-way grade and feedback passback eliminates manual data entry, saving hundreds of teacher hours per semester.

Read more in how Checkmark writing process analysis works .
```

Legacy LTI 1.1 pop-up window architecture.

Certified LTI 1.3 embedded natively in SpeedGrader. 
 Plagiarism + AI + 15-second Keystroke Video Playback. 
 Objective drafting proof that protects honest students. 
 Predictable, transparent district licensing with unlimited scans.

### Section: A 5-Step Technology Director Procurement Protocol
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `e1bab6c2e966da1909062e629e8355dd9f4ca4f4c07e2e8767d56ac7e8ea32d9`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/what-is-hecvat-and-why-does-it-matter-when-buying-edtech` (Similarity: 0.8146)

```text
EdTech Procurement Protocol:

1. Audit vendor compliance: require completed HECVAT, SOC 2 Type II, and signed NDPA. 
 2. Test LMS integration in a sandbox: verify LTI 1.3 SpeedGrader embedding and grade passback. 
 3. Evaluate detection capabilities: test live web matching, institutional peer repositories, and video playback. 
 4. Review administrative dashboards: verify course anomaly heatmaps and longitudinal tracking. 
 5. Launch a 30-day pilot across selected departments before full district-wide deployment.
```
